@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.poincare_hilbert : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  Module.Free Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial
      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) ∧
    Module.Free Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial
        (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) ∧
      Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.rankPolynomial ideal =
          Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal ∧
        Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.rankPolynomial ideal =
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal ∧
          Polynomial.reverse (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal) =
              Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal ∧
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.hilbertSeries ideal =
              ↑(Polynomial.map (Nat.castRingHom ℚ) (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal)) *
                ↑(PowerSeries.invOneSubPow ℚ 2)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.poincare_hilbert' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.basis_explicit : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  ∃ values,
    (∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n), values vertex = Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex) ∧
      (∃ vectors, ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n), vectors vertex = values vertex) ∧
        (∃ vectors, ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n), vectors vertex = values vertex) ∧
          ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
            values vertex ∈
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.piece (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                  (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    vertex) ∧
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.degree (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    (values vertex) =
                  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    vertex ∧
                (values vertex =
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.restrictFull
                        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline n hthree vertex) ∨
                  Nonempty (Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm ideal vertex (values vertex)))
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.basis_explicit' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex =

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm.formula : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  {ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree} {vertex : DihedralGroup n}
  {value : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)}
  (self : Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm ideal vertex value) (current : DihedralGroup n),
  ↑value current =
    if current ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm.vertices self then
      MvPolynomial.C (Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm.coefficient self current) *
        Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryProduct (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm.vertices self) current
    else 0
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.BoundaryForm.formula' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  {ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree} {vertex : DihedralGroup n}
  {value : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)}

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.graded_class : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ≠
      Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n →
    ∀ (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
      alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∧ beta = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 ∨
          alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 ∧ beta = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 →
        (Odd
              (Set.ncard
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))) →
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.IsDominant ideal alpha) →
          have half :=
            Set.ncard
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) /
              2;
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
              hthree =
            if
                Even
                  (Set.ncard
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))) then
              (1 + PowerSeries.X) * ∑ exponent ∈ Finset.range (2 * half), PowerSeries.X ^ exponent +
                PowerSeries.X ^ half *
                  PowerSeries.C
                    (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep alpha) +
                          TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep beta) -
                        TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n half) -
                      TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n (half - 1)))
            else
              (1 + PowerSeries.X) * ∑ exponent ∈ Finset.range (2 * half + 1), PowerSeries.X ^ exponent +
                (PowerSeries.X ^ half + PowerSeries.X ^ (half + 1)) *
                  PowerSeries.C
                    (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep alpha) -
                      TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n half))
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.graded_class' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
      2 * half →
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ≠
        Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n →

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.graded_class : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ≠
      Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n →
    ∀ (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
      alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∧ beta = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 ∨
          alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 ∧ beta = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 →
        (Odd
              (Set.ncard
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))) →
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.IsDominant ideal alpha) →
          have half :=
            Set.ncard
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) /
              2;
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Right.gradedClass (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
              hthree =
            if
                Even
                  (Set.ncard
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))) then
              Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass ideal +
                PowerSeries.X ^ half *
                  PowerSeries.C
                    (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep alpha) +
                          TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep beta) -
                        TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n half) -
                      TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n (half - 1)))

                  PowerSeries.X ^ half *
                    PowerSeries.C
                      (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep alpha) -
                        TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n half)) +
                PowerSeries.X ^ (half + 1) *
                  PowerSeries.C
                    (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep beta) -

Schubert.Dihedral.numbered_even_reflection : ∀ (n exponent : ℕ) (hbound : 2 * exponent < n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection ((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv n) ⟨2 * exponent, hbound⟩) =
    (Schubert.Dihedral.firstSimpleReflection n * Schubert.Dihedral.secondSimpleReflection n) ^ exponent *
      Schubert.Dihedral.firstSimpleReflection n
'Schubert.Dihedral.numbered_even_reflection' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.Dihedral.numbered_odd_reflection : ∀ (n exponent : ℕ) (hbound : 2 * exponent + 1 < n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection ((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv n) ⟨2 * exponent + 1, hbound⟩) =
    (Schubert.Dihedral.secondSimpleReflection n * Schubert.Dihedral.firstSimpleReflection n) ^ exponent *
      Schubert.Dihedral.secondSimpleReflection n
'Schubert.Dihedral.numbered_odd_reflection' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  first ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.roots n →
    second ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.roots n → ∀ (scalar : ℝ), first = scalar • second → scalar = 1 ∨ scalar = -1

  normal ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.roots n →
    vector ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.roots n →
      vector - (2 * Schubert.Dihedral.realBilinearForm vector normal) • normal ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.roots n

@Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.unique_nearest_pair : ∀ {n : ℕ} (data : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator n),

    ∃! pair,
      Finset.card pair = 2 ∧
        ↑pair ⊆ Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.positive data ∧
          ∀ first ∈ pair,
            ∀ second ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.positive data,
              second ∉ pair →
                Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.distance data first < Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.distance data second

          Schubert.Dihedral.realBilinearForm (vector - point) (vector - point)

@Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.nearest_pair : ∀ {n : ℕ} {data : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator n}
  (frame : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame data) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (pair : Finset Schubert.Dihedral.RealPlane),
  (Finset.card pair = 2 ∧
      ↑pair ⊆ Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.positive data ∧
        ∀ first ∈ pair,
          ∀ second ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.positive data,
            second ∉ pair →
              Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.distance data first < Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.distance data second) →
    pair =
      {↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.first frame hthree),
        ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.second frame hthree)}

Schubert.Dihedral.leftInversionSet_descent : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (element : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.leftInversionSet n element =
    Subtype.val ''
      {alpha |
        Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) <
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element}

Schubert.Dihedral.unique_root_descent : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (element : DihedralGroup n),
  element ≠ 1 →
    element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree →
      ∃! alpha,
        (alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element - 1
'Schubert.Dihedral.unique_root_descent' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

    element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree →
      ∃! alpha,
        (alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1

Schubert.Dihedral.ncard_leftInversionSet : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (element : DihedralGroup n),

@Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.left_ascent : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 →
        Schubert.Dihedral.leftInversionSet n (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
          insert (↑alpha)
            (Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) ''
              Schubert.Dihedral.leftInversionSet n element)

  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 →

          Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) ''
            Schubert.Dihedral.leftInversionSet n element

@Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.right_ascent : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 →
        Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n element⁻¹ ↑alpha ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.rightInversionSet n (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
              insert (Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n element⁻¹ ↑alpha) (Schubert.Dihedral.rightInversionSet n element) ∧
            Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n element⁻¹ ↑alpha ∉ Schubert.Dihedral.rightInversionSet n element

Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal : (n : ℕ) → 3 ≤ n → Type
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.classification : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  (∃ size,
      2 ≤ size ∧
        size ≤ n ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < size}) ∨
    ∃ layer,
      2 ≤ layer ∧
        layer ≤ n / 2 ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * layer - 2 ∨ ↑index = 2 * layer - 1}
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.classification' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.exists_initial : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (size : ℕ),
  2 ≤ size →
    size ≤ n →
      ∃ ideal,
        Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
            size ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < size}
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.exists_initial' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.exists_other : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (layer : ℕ),
  2 ≤ layer →
    layer ≤ n / 2 →
      ∃ ideal,
        Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
            2 * layer - 1 ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * layer - 2 ∨ ↑index = 2 * layer - 1}
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.exists_other' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.natCard : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n),
  Nat.card (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) = n + n / 2 - 2
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.natCard' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

@Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.depth_layer : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.depth hthree alpha = ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) / 2 + 1

@Schubert.Dihedral.depth_step_iff : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.DepthStep hthree alpha beta ↔
    ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) % 2 = 0 ∧
        ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index beta) = ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) + 3 ∨
      ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) % 2 ≠ 0 ∧
          ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index beta) = ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) + 1 ∨
        n % 2 = 1 ∧
          ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index alpha) = n - 3 ∧ ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index beta) = n - 1
'Schubert.Dihedral.depth_step_iff' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.Dihedral.depth_order_iff : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  Relation.ReflTransGen (Schubert.Dihedral.DepthStep hthree) alpha beta ↔
    Relation.ReflTransGen
      (fun first second =>
        ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index first) % 2 = 0 ∧
            ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index second) = ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index first) + 3 ∨
          ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index first) % 2 ≠ 0 ∧
              ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index second) = ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index first) + 1 ∨
            n % 2 = 1 ∧
              ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index first) = n - 3 ∧
                ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.index second) = n - 1)
      alpha beta

      {Schubert.Dihedral.linearPolynomial

            (Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n vertex ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.choose data alpha)))} =

@Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.spline_signs : ∀ {n : ℕ} (data : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator n) (hthree : 3 ≤ n)
  (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset n) (value : DihedralGroup n → Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.IsSpline selected value ↔
    ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Positive data),
      ↑alpha ∈ (fun beta => ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.choose data beta)) '' {beta | ↑beta ∈ selected} →
        Schubert.Dihedral.linearPolynomial
            (Schubert.Dihedral.complexify (Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n vertex ↑alpha)) ∣
          value vertex - value (vertex * ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.reflectionEquiv data hthree) alpha))

Schubert.DihedralSplines.six_depth_counterexample : Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots ⊆
    Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots 6 ∧
  Set.ncard Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots = 4 ∧
    ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot 6 ⋯ 0) ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots ∧
      ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot 6 ⋯ 1) ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots ∧
        (∀ (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot 6),
            Relation.ReflTransGen (Schubert.Dihedral.DepthStep ⋯) alpha beta →
              ↑beta ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots → ↑alpha ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.sixLowerRoots) ∧
          ∀ (data : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator 6) (frame : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame data),
            ¬((∀ (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Positive data),
                  (∃ first second,
                      0 ≤ first ∧
                        0 ≤ second ∧
                          ↑beta - ↑alpha =
                            first • ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.first frame ⋯) +
                              second • ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.second frame ⋯)) →
                    ↑beta ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.sixChamberRoots data →
                      ↑alpha ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.sixChamberRoots data) ∧
                ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.first frame ⋯) ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.sixChamberRoots data ∧
                  ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.second frame ⋯) ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.sixChamberRoots data)

  ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.first frame ⋯) = -Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 4 ∧
    ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.Frame.second frame ⋯) = Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 2 ∧
      Schubert.Dihedral.Separator.positive data =
          {Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 0, Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 2, -Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 4,

            3 ∧

                  0 ≤ first ∧

                      -Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot 6 4} →
                  ↑alpha ∈

  Schubert.Dihedral.RootLattice.Lower Schubert.Dihedral.RootLattice.a2 (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier selected) ↔
    ∀ (alpha beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot 3),
      Schubert.DihedralSplines.RootOrder 3 ⋯ alpha beta → ↑beta ∈ selected → ↑alpha ∈ selected

@Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.simple_reachable : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier selected =
      {↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0), ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)} →
    ∀ (source target : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.reachable hthree selected source target ↔
        Schubert.Dihedral.RightWeakLE n hthree source target

Schubert.Dihedral.rightWeakLE_prefix : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (source target : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RightWeakLE n hthree source target ↔
    ∃ first second,
      CoxeterSystem.IsReduced (Schubert.Dihedral.coxeterSystem n hthree) (first ++ second) ∧
        source = CoxeterSystem.wordProd (Schubert.Dihedral.coxeterSystem n hthree) first ∧
          target = CoxeterSystem.wordProd (Schubert.Dihedral.coxeterSystem n hthree) (first ++ second)
'Schubert.Dihedral.rightWeakLE_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

Schubert.DihedralSplines.pentagon_oriented : ∀ (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset 5),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier selected =
      {↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨0, ⋯⟩), ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨1, ⋯⟩),
        ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨2, ⋯⟩)} →
    have a := Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot 5 ⋯ 0);
    have b := Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot 5 ⋯ 1);
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.orientedEdges ⋯ selected =
      {(1, a), (1, b), (1, a * b * a), (a, a * b), (a, b * a), (a * b, a * b * a), (a * b, a * b * a * b * a),
        (a * b * a, a * b * a * b), (a * b * a * b, a * b * a * b * a), (b * a * b * a, a * b * a * b * a),
        (b * a * b, b * a * b * a), (b * a * b, a * b * a * b), (b * a, b * a * b), (b, b * a), (b, b * a * b * a)}

Schubert.DihedralSplines.pentagon_cover_iff : ∀ (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset 5),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier selected =
      {↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨0, ⋯⟩), ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨1, ⋯⟩),
        ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨2, ⋯⟩)} →
    ∀ (source target : DihedralGroup 5),
      source ⋖ target ↔
        (source, target) ∈ Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.orientedEdges ⋯ selected ∧
          Schubert.Dihedral.length 5 ⋯ target = Schubert.Dihedral.length 5 ⋯ source + 1

      {↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨0, ⋯⟩), ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv 5) ⟨1, ⋯⟩),

      Schubert.DihedralSplines.SquareIllustrations.value diagram (Prod.snd (Schubert.DihedralSplines.Square.pairs row))

@Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edgeLabel_eq_form_iff : ∀ {n : ℕ} [NeZero n] (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset n)
  (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n) (hmem : ↑alpha ∈ selected)
  (beta : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edgeLabel selected (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edge selected vertex alpha hmem) =
        Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form beta ∨
      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edgeLabel selected (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edge selected vertex alpha hmem) =
        -Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form beta ↔
    Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection beta * vertex = vertex * Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha

  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree)
  (generators :
    Set (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))),
  (∀ (value : ↑generators),
      ∃ degree,
        ↑value ∈
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.piece (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) degree) →
    Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial generators = ⊤ →
      (∀ smaller ⊆ generators,
          Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial smaller = ⊤ → smaller = generators) →
        ∀ (degree : ℕ),
          Nat.card
              { value //
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.degree (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    ↑value =
                  degree } =

              ↥(Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.piece (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                  hthree degree)

        ↑value ∈

      Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial first = ⊤ →
        Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial second = ⊤ →
          (∀ smaller ⊆ first, Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial smaller = ⊤ → smaller = first) →
            (∀ smaller ⊆ second, Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial smaller = ⊤ → smaller = second) →

    {E : Type u_3} →

@Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.upperAt_ideal : ∀ {R : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} {E : Type u_3} [inst : CommRing R]
  [inst_1 : PartialOrder V] (graph : Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph R V E)
  (value : Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.Spline graph) (vertex : V),
  Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.IsUpperAt graph value vertex ↔
    Schubert.Splines.IdealLabeledGraph.IsUpperAt (Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.idealGraph graph)
      ((Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.idealEquiv graph) value) vertex

  (family : V → Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.Spline graph),
  Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.IsUpperFamily graph family ↔
    Schubert.Splines.IdealLabeledGraph.IsUpperFamily (Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.idealGraph graph) fun vertex =>
      (Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.idealEquiv graph) (family vertex)

@Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.upper_matrix : ∀ {R : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} {E : Type u_3} [inst : CommRing R]
  [inst_1 : PartialOrder V] (graph : Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph R V E) {I : Type u_4} [inst_2 : LinearOrder I]
  (order : I ≃ V),
  (∀ (first second : V), first ≤ second → (Equiv.symm order) first ≤ (Equiv.symm order) second) →
    ∀ (family : V → Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.Spline graph),
      Schubert.Splines.PolynomialGraph.IsUpperFamily graph family →
        Matrix.IsUpperTriangular fun first second => ↑(family (order first)) (order second)

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.TriangularFamily.Left.minimal : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]

  (vertices : Set (DihedralGroup n)),
  Submodule.span Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial
        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.TriangularFamily.value family '' vertices) =
      ⊤ ↔
    vertices = Set.univ

  (vertex : DihedralGroup n),

    ((Schubert.Dihedral.polynomialAction n) element) (↑value (element⁻¹ * vertex))

Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full_iff_left : ∀ (n : ℕ),
  3 ≤ n →
    ∀ (value : DihedralGroup n → Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial),
      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.IsSpline (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full n) value ↔
        ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
          Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha ∣
            value vertex - value (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex)
'Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full_iff_left' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full_iff_left : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n)
  (value : DihedralGroup n → Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial),
  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.IsSpline
      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full n hthree))
      value ↔
    ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha ∣
        value vertex - value (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full_iff_left' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.splineAlgebra (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full n) =
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.leftSplineAlgebra n hthree

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.difference : {n : ℕ} →
  {hthree : 3 ≤ n} →
    [NeZero n] →
      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline
          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset
            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full n hthree)) →
        Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n →
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline
            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset
              (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full n hthree))

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.difference_mul : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (value :
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline
      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.full n hthree)))
  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha * ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.difference value alpha) vertex =
    ↑value vertex -
      ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.dot (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full n) hthree
            (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) value)
        vertex

  ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.top n hthree) vertex =
    if vertex = Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree then ∏ alpha, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha else 0

Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline_step : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) [inst : NeZero n]
  (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (hneOne : vertex ≠ 1) (hneTop : vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree),
  have alpha := Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot n hthree vertex hneOne hneTop;
  Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline n hthree vertex =
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.difference
      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline n hthree
        (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex))
      alpha
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline_step' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot_spec : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (vertex : DihedralGroup n)
  (hneOne : vertex ≠ 1) (hneTop : vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree),
  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot n hthree vertex hneOne hneTop =
        Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨
      Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot n hthree vertex hneOne hneTop =
        Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) ∧
    Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree
        (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection
            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot n hthree vertex hneOne hneTop) *
          vertex) =
      Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot_spec' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot_eq : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (vertex : DihedralGroup n)
  (hneOne : vertex ≠ 1) (hneTop : vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree)
  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) =
        Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 →
      Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot n hthree vertex hneOne hneTop = alpha
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.ascentRoot_eq' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (vertex : DihedralGroup n),

    (∀ (current : DihedralGroup n), coefficient current ≠ 0 ↔ Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree vertex current) ∧

      (AlgHom.toRingHom

        (FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial)
        (FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial) ≃ₐ[FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial]
      DihedralGroup n → FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial
'Schubert.Dihedral.fixedTensorEquiv' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.Dihedral.fixedTensorEquiv_tmul : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n]
  (first second : FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial) (element : DihedralGroup n),
  (Schubert.Dihedral.fixedTensorEquiv n)
      (first ⊗ₜ[↥(FixedPoints.subfield (DihedralGroup n) (FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial))] second)
      element =
    first * element • second
'Schubert.Dihedral.fixedTensorEquiv_tmul' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (fraction : FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial)
  (polynomial : Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial),
  (Schubert.Dihedral.polynomialFixedEquiv n) (fraction ⊗ₜ[↥(Schubert.Dihedral.invariantSubalgebra n)] polynomial) =

  (fraction : FractionRing Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial) (polynomial : Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial)
  (element : DihedralGroup n),

      element =

Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.right_class_coeff : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n] (hthree : 3 ≤ n) (degree : ℕ),
  (PowerSeries.coeff degree)
      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Right.gradedClass (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full n) hthree) =
    if degree = 0 then TauCeti.SplitK0.of (FDRep.of (Representation.trivial ℂ (DihedralGroup n) ℂ))
    else
      if degree = n then TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.signFDRep n)
      else if degree < n then TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n ↑degree) else 0
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.right_class_coeff' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

Schubert.DihedralSplines.DoubleCoinvariant.localization_image : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n] (hthree : 3 ≤ n)
  (value : DihedralGroup n → Schubert.Dihedral.CoordinatePolynomial),
  (∃ polynomial, ↑((Schubert.DihedralSplines.DoubleCoinvariant.localization n hthree) polynomial) = value) ↔
    ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha ∣
        value vertex - value (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex)

 Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.DoubleCoinvariant.localization_inverse : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n] (hthree : 3 ≤ n)

  ↑((Schubert.DihedralSplines.DoubleCoinvariant.localization n hthree)
          ((Ideal.Quotient.mk (Schubert.Dihedral.DoubleCoinvariant.relations n)) polynomial))
      vertex =
    (MvPolynomial.aeval (Sum.elim MvPolynomial.X (Schubert.Dihedral.inverseCoordinate n vertex))) polynomial

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.DoubleCoinvariant.root_form_real : ∀ {n : ℕ} (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha =
    (MvPolynomial.map Complex.ofRealHom)
      (MvPolynomial.C (↑alpha 0) * MvPolynomial.X 0 + MvPolynomial.C (↑alpha 1) * MvPolynomial.X 1)

Schubert.Dihedral.inverseCoordinate_eval : ∀ (n : ℕ) (element : DihedralGroup n) (coordinate : Fin 2)
  (point : Schubert.Dihedral.ComplexPlane),
  (MvPolynomial.eval point) (Schubert.Dihedral.inverseCoordinate n element coordinate) =
    (Schubert.Dihedral.vectorOperator n element⁻¹) point coordinate
'Schubert.Dihedral.inverseCoordinate_eval' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference : {n : ℕ} →
  {hthree : 3 ≤ n} →
    (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) →
      (value : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) →
        (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n) →
          (∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
              ↑value vertex ≠ 0 →
                (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
                  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) →
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference_mul : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree)
  (value : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n)
  (hedges :
    ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
      ↑value vertex ≠ 0 →
        (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
  (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha *
      ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference ideal value alpha hedges) vertex =
    ↑value vertex -
      ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.dot (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) hthree
            (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) value)
        vertex
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference_mul' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference_unique : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree)
  (value : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n)
  (hedges :
    ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
      ↑value vertex ≠ 0 →
        (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
  (quotient : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)),
  (∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.form alpha * ↑quotient vertex =
        ↑value vertex -
          ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.dot (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) hthree
                (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) value)
            vertex) →
    quotient = Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference ideal value alpha hedges

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
@Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.ascent_length : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n)
  (alpha gamma : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree element (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) →
        element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element →
          Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element =
              element * Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection gamma →
            Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection gamma) =
              if 2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 ≤ n then
                2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1
              else 2 * (n - Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element) - 1
'Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.ascent_length' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.descent_length : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hthree : 3 ≤ n)
  (alpha gamma : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) element →
        Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element ≠ element →
          Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element =
              element * Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection gamma →
            Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection gamma) =
              if 2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element - 1 ≤ n then
                2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element - 1
              else 2 * (n - Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element) + 1
'Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.descent_length' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.inversions_no_edge : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree element (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) →
        element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element →
          (element, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) ∉
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.inversions (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) =
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.inversions (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                element

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.length_edge : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree element (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) →
        element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element →
          (element, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) ∈
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) element +
                1 =
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.length_edge' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.edge_range : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree element (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) →
        element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element →
          (element, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) ∈
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
            if 2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 ≤ n then
              ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
                Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex ≤ Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element →
                  (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
                    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
            else
              ∀ (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
                Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element < Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex →
                  (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
                    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.edge_range' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.length_full : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    ∀ (element : DihedralGroup n),
      Schubert.Dihedral.BruhatLE n hthree element (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) →
        element ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element →
          2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element + 1 ≤ n →
            (element, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * element) ∈
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                  element =
                Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree element

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.topSpline : {n : ℕ} →
  {hthree : 3 ≤ n} →
    [NeZero n] →
      (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) →
        Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Spline (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.topSpline' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.topSpline_apply : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (current : DihedralGroup n),
  ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.topSpline ideal) current =
    if current = Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree then
      ∏ alpha ∈ Set.Finite.toFinset ⋯,
        Schubert.Dihedral.linearPolynomial
          (Schubert.Dihedral.complexify
            (Schubert.Dihedral.realAction n (Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree) alpha))
    else 0
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.topSpline_apply' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline_low : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  vertex ≠ 1 →
    vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree →
      ∀ (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
        alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) =
              Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 →
            (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
              2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 ≤ n →
                Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex =
                  (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.restrictFull
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
                    (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline n hthree vertex)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline_low' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline_high : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  vertex ≠ 1 →
    vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree →
      ∀ (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n)
        (hsimple : alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)
        (hascent :
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) =
            Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1)
        (hedge :
          (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
        (hhalf : n < 2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1),
        Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex =
          Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.dividedDifference ideal
            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal
              (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex))
            alpha ⋯
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline_high' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline_no_edge : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  vertex ≠ 1 →
    vertex ≠ Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree →
      ∀ (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
        alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) =
              Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 →
            (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∉
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
              Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex =

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial_even : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (half : ℕ),
  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
      2 * half →
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal =
      (1 + Polynomial.X) * ∑ exponent ∈ Finset.range (2 * half), Polynomial.X ^ exponent +
        Polynomial.C (2 * n - 4 * half) * Polynomial.X ^ half
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial_even' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (half : ℕ),
  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
      2 * half + 1 →
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal =
      (1 + Polynomial.X) * ∑ exponent ∈ Finset.range (2 * half + 1), Polynomial.X ^ exponent +
        Polynomial.C (n - 2 * half - 1) * (Polynomial.X ^ half + Polynomial.X ^ (half + 1))

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.hilbertSeries_formula : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
  Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.hilbertSeries ideal =
    ↑(Polynomial.map (Nat.castRingHom ℚ) (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal)) *
      ↑(PowerSeries.invOneSubPow ℚ 2)

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coeff_piecewise : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (degree : ℕ),
  (Polynomial.coeff (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.lengthPolynomial ideal)) degree =
    if
        Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) <
          degree then
      0
    else
      if
          degree = 0 ∨
            degree =
              Set.ncard
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) then
        1
      else
        if
            degree <
                Set.ncard
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) /
                  2 ∨
              Set.ncard
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) -
                  Set.ncard
                      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) /
                    2 <
                degree then
          2
        else
          if
              Even
                (Set.ncard
                  (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                    (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))) then
            2 * n -
                2 *
                  Set.ncard
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) +
              2
          else
            n -
                Set.ncard
                  (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) +
              2
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coeff_piecewise' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.first_regular : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n) (half : ℕ)
  (hbound : 2 * half < n),
  ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv n) ⟨2 * half, hbound⟩) ∈ ideal →
    ∀
      source ∈
        Schubert.Dihedral.alternatingBlock vertex (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)
          (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) half,
      Set.ncard
          {target |
            (source, target) ∈
                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ∧
              target ∈
                Schubert.Dihedral.alternatingBlock vertex (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)
                  (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) half} =
        half + 1
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.first_regular' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.edge : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  {ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree}
  (block : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block ideal) (first second : ℕ),
  first < 2 * Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.half block + 2 →
    second < 2 * Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.half block + 2 →
      ((Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block *
              Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.alternatingProduct
                (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.alpha block)
                (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.beta block) first,
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block *
              Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.alternatingProduct
                (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.alpha block)
                (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.beta block) second) ∈
          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ↔
        first % 2 ≠ second % 2)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.edge' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (block : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block ideal) {A : Sort u_1} (value : DihedralGroup n → A),

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.localized : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [NeZero n]
  {ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree}
  (block : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block ideal),
  ∀
    value ∈
      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.piece (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
        (Finset.card
          (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryRoots (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
            (↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block))
            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block))),
    (∀ vertex ∉ Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block, ↑value vertex = 0) →
      value ≠ 0 →

          ∃ scalar,
            scalar ≠ 0 ∧
              ↑value vertex =
                MvPolynomial.C scalar *
                  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryProduct
                    (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    (↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block)) vertex

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.block_shape : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex =
      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.restrictFull (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal))
        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.SchubertSpline.spline n hthree vertex) ∨
    vertex = Schubert.Dihedral.longestElement n hthree ∨
      ∃ block,
        Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block = vertex ∧
          Finset.card
                (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryRoots
                  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                  (↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block))
                  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block)) =
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) vertex ∧
            ∀ (current : DihedralGroup n),
              ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex) current ≠ 0 ↔
                current ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.block_shape' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.high_block : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n)
  (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
  alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0 ∨ alpha = Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1 →
    Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) =
        Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 →
      n < 2 * Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree vertex + 1 →
        (vertex, Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha * vertex) ∈
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.edges (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) →
          ∃ block,
            Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block = vertex ∧
              Finset.card
                    (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryRoots
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                      (↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block))
                      (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.base block)) =
                  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
                    vertex ∧
                ∀ (current : DihedralGroup n),
                  ↑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex) current ≠ 0 ↔
                    current ∈ Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.high_block' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.homogeneous : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (vertex : DihedralGroup n),
  Schubert.DihedralSplines.FlowupSpline.spline ideal vertex ∈
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.piece (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)
      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.length (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) vertex)

@Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.independent : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} (selected : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset n)
  (half : ℕ),
  2 * half + 1 < n →
    (∀ (alpha : Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot n),
        ↑alpha ∈ selected ↔
          Schubert.Dihedral.length n hthree (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.reflection alpha) ≤ 2 * half + 1) →
      ∀ (coefficient : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.AlternatingBlocks n hthree half → DihedralGroup n → ℂ),
        (∀ (block : Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.AlternatingBlocks n hthree half),
            ∀ vertex ∈ ↑block, coefficient block vertex ≠ 0) →
          LinearIndependent ℂ fun block =>
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.boundaryFunction selected (↑↑block) (coefficient block)

'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.AdmissibleBlocks' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.admissible_iff : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (half : ℕ) (hbound : 2 * half + 1 < n)
  (vertices : Finset (DihedralGroup n)),
  (∃ block,
      Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.half block = half ∧
        Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Block.vertices block = vertices) ↔
    (↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv n) ⟨2 * half, ⋯⟩) ∈ ideal ∧
        ∃ base,
          vertices =
            Schubert.Dihedral.alternatingBlock base (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)
              (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1) half) ∨
      ↑((Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRootEquiv n) ⟨2 * half + 1, hbound⟩) ∈ ideal ∧
        ∃ base,
          vertices =
            Schubert.Dihedral.alternatingBlock base (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)
              (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0) half

      2 * half + 1 →

        Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n →

Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.odd_choices : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n),
  Even n →
    ∀ (half : ℕ),
      1 ≤ half →
        2 * half + 1 < n →
          ∃ first second,
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) =
                Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * half + 1} ∧
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) =
                  Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * half ∨ ↑index = 2 * half + 1} ∧
                Set.ncard
                      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first)) =
                    2 * half + 1 ∧
                  Set.ncard
                        (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier
                          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second)) =
                      2 * half + 1 ∧
                    ¬Nonempty
                          (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.pieceFDRep
                              (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree half ≅
                            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.pieceFDRep
                              (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree half) ∧
                      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass
                            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree ≠
                          Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass
                            (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree ∧
                        ¬∀ (degree : ℕ),
                            Nonempty
                              (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedRepresentation
                                  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree degree ≅
                                Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedRepresentation
                                  (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree degree)
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.odd_choices' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.odd_choice : ∀ (n : ℕ) (hthree : 3 ≤ n),
  Even n →
    ∀ (half : ℕ),
      1 ≤ half →
        2 * half + 1 < n →
          ∀ (first second : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree),
            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) =
                Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * half + 1} →
              Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) =
                  Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoot n '' {index | ↑index < 2 * half ∨ ↑index = 2 * half + 1} →
                ¬Nonempty
                      (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.pieceFDRep
                          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree half ≅
                        Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.pieceFDRep
                          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree half) ∧
                  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass
                        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree ≠
                      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass
                        (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree ∧
                    ¬∀ (degree : ℕ),
                        Nonempty
                          (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedRepresentation
                              (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset first) hthree degree ≅
                            Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedRepresentation
                              (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset second) hthree degree)

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.dimension_even : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) (half : ℕ),
  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =

      Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.full n) hthree =

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.two_simple_middle : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n],
  TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)) +
          TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)) -
        TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n 1) -
      TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.Frequency.partialSum n 0) =
    TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)) +
          TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)) -
        TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n 1) -
      2

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Left.two_simple_class : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]

  Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) =
      {↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0), ↑(Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)} →
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.Left.gradedClass (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) hthree =
      1 +
          PowerSeries.C
              (2 +
                      TauCeti.SplitK0.of
                        (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 0)) +
                    TauCeti.SplitK0.of
                      (Schubert.Dihedral.PositiveRoot.cosetFDRep (Schubert.Dihedral.simpleRoot n hthree 1)) -
                  TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n 1) -
                2) *
            PowerSeries.X +
        PowerSeries.X ^ 2

      (degree :

      Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) →

          value

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.co_subrepresentation : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n} [inst : NeZero n]
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree)
  (hproper :
    Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal) ≠
      Schubert.Dihedral.positiveRoots n),
  (∀ (degree : ℕ),
      Function.Injective ⇑(Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.coInclusion ideal hproper degree)) ∧
    (∀ (degree : ℕ),
        Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) <
            degree →
          Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.coRange ideal hproper degree = ⊥) ∧
      Schubert.FiniteGroupCharacters.gradedClass
          (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.Right.coGradedRepresentation ideal hproper) =
        Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass ideal

@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass : {n : ℕ} →
  {hthree : 3 ≤ n} →
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree → PowerSeries (TauCeti.repRing ℂ (DihedralGroup n))
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass_even : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) [inst : NeZero n] (half : ℕ),
  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
      2 * half →
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass ideal =
      (PowerSeries.monomial 0) (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (FDRep.of (Representation.trivial ℂ (DihedralGroup n) ℂ))) +
            ∑ index ∈ Finset.range half,
              (PowerSeries.monomial (1 + index))
                (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n ↑(index + 1))) +
          ∑ index ∈ Finset.range (half - 1),
            (PowerSeries.monomial (half + 1 + index))
              (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n ↑(half - 1 - index))) +
        (PowerSeries.monomial (2 * half)) (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.signFDRep n))
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass_even' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass_odd : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hthree : 3 ≤ n}
  (ideal : Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal n hthree) [inst : NeZero n] (half : ℕ),
  Set.ncard (Schubert.Dihedral.RootSubset.carrier (Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.toRootSubset ideal)) =
      2 * half + 1 →
    Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass ideal =
      (PowerSeries.monomial 0) (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (FDRep.of (Representation.trivial ℂ (DihedralGroup n) ℂ))) +
            ∑ index ∈ Finset.range half,
              (PowerSeries.monomial (1 + index))
                (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n ↑(index + 1))) +
          ∑ index ∈ Finset.range half,
            (PowerSeries.monomial (half + 1 + index))
              (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.frequencyFDRep n ↑(half - index))) +
        (PowerSeries.monomial (2 * half + 1)) (TauCeti.SplitK0.of (Schubert.Dihedral.signFDRep n))
'Schubert.DihedralSplines.HessenbergIdeal.coClass_odd' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
